# Documentation drift audit

The documents mix working implementations, old snapshots and future designs. The most consequential mismatches concern save version 25, active water features, implemented menus and freshness handling, and architecture names that do not exist in the code. The ranked table below records 50 misleading claims with baseline evidence. Several ADRs still say Proposed although related code is implemented; that is a documentation mismatch, not proof of formal approval.

**Baseline:** `81b0795db6bd3bd6a5352d66167d4863f0ae2f41`. All line numbers and existence checks refer to this commit, not a moving remote branch. Only this audit document changes. No source, scene, asset, configuration or save was changed.

## Scope and reading rules

- Read all 19 scoped narrative documents: `docs/roadmap.md`, every Markdown file under `backlog/`, the nine top-level `docs/architecture/*.md` files, and `agents/coordination.md` (5,578 lines). Architecture subdirectories were outside the requested glob.
- Read 61 status paragraphs across 59 ADR files, including two amendments and the draft proposal. ADR bodies were not audited.
- Checked state assertions against tracked source, serialized data, assembly references, the build list and CI configuration. Proposed acceptance criteria remain proposals; a dated “this PR” scope statement is not automatically a claim about current main.
- A declaration proves a symbol exists, not that its behavior passes a test. A scene reference or material value proves committed wiring, not visible quality. Historical playtest verdicts, timings and counts cannot be reproduced from source; those are marked unverified below.
- The rank reflects likely implementation harm: incorrect rules/schema first, duplicate work and false activation gates next, misleading navigation and status labels after that. Related occurrences are grouped rather than padding the ranking with duplicates.
- Evidence links are relative to this document. Shortened link labels omit `Assets/_Project/Code/` or `Assets/_Project/`; destinations retain the full tracked path. Negative type/file findings come from the complete baseline tree and runtime C# declaration scan, with the actual replacement cited when known.

Copying the document would invert wet/dry and grounding decisions. |

## Other checked claims and boundaries

These checks retain useful, current documentation and separate historical evidence or future acceptance from a present-state claim. “Corroborated” means the cited source/data supports the stated fact; it is not a fresh test result. “Unverified” records the limit rather than inventing a pass.

| Source | Claim checked | Result and baseline evidence |
|---|---|---|
| [docs/roadmap.md:26](../docs/roadmap.md#L26) | M1 GO verdict and owner playtest history. | Unverified historical verdict. The build list ([ProjectSettings/EditorBuildSettings.asset:8](../ProjectSettings/EditorBuildSettings.asset#L8)) establishes committed scenes, not a human verdict or readiness report. No acceptance was revoked or newly granted. |
| [docs/roadmap.md:268](../docs/roadmap.md#L268); [backlog/backlog.md:342](../backlog/backlog.md#L342) | Milestone “Systems built” lists and M2–M4 acceptance rows. | Roadmap deliverables describe intended phase scope, not a reliable shipped checklist. Read the explicit status/date of each row. Current declarations include [Boats/BoatController.cs:19](../Assets/_Project/Code/Boats/BoatController.cs#L19), [Economy/Market.cs:17](../Assets/_Project/Code/Economy/Market.cs#L17) and [Core/Crew/BoatVoyageDef.cs:44](../Assets/_Project/Code/Core/Crew/BoatVoyageDef.cs#L44); their existence does not imply rescue, all business simulation, freight or co-op is complete. |

## Verification and limits

- Baseline tree inventory, local link resolution and source/data citations were checked without fetching a different revision. All generated citations name tracked files and in-range lines at the pinned baseline.
- Fifty ranked rows, the additional claim ledger, all 61 ADR status paragraphs, absent/moved path classifications and the symbol/member inventory are included. A checked path/name does not prove a promised acceptance criterion; future designs and historical measurements are explicitly distinguished.
- No new test or reusable validator was introduced. Existing content, identity, routine, facet-budget and sorting tests were located as evidence; source presence is not a passing test run.
- No Unity editor, EditMode/PlayMode suite, build, managed-test harness, graphics comparison, audio playback or performance benchmark was run. No current CI duration, remote branch-protection setting, review approval or historical human verdict was verified.
- Only this document is proposed. The stale source documents, ADR statuses, assets, source and save schema remain unchanged.
